# Test inventory: mycel

[Execution policy](../TESTS.md) · [Mid-tier descriptions and reasons](../MID_TIER.md) · [Machine-readable inventory](../test-tiers.json).

Folder separation from `faff2f949f25e3c486cf314e881a83a5a689a1ef` on 29 October 2026. Original source addresses remain in test-tiers.json. Top cases keep their previous individual justifications; provisional mid classifications remain provisional.

## Top tier

| Selection | State | Justification |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [`IngestStepStoreTests.test_a_series_completes_while_a_file_changes_before_every_step`](../tests/top/test_top_ingest.py#L8) | active | Documented SYL-315 regression of SYL-289 run 3: while another agent edited a file before every step, each step that wrote it started the refresh of unchanged files again, so a stepped pass never completed. Edits a file before every step of a series and requires the pass to complete within a bounded number of steps. |
| [`IngestStepStoreTests.test_a_refreshed_file_resting_on_a_written_file_is_refreshed_again`](../tests/top/test_top_ingest.py#L29) | active | Non-obvious edge of the SYL-315 fix: a file the refresh already passed rests, through a Source Ref, on a section a later step writes. Requires that step to refresh it again, and the finished series to leave the dependency, edge, relation and stale rows exactly as one whole pass does. |
| [`RecoveryStoreTests.test_edit_during_final_audit_rolls_back_the_origin_claim_and_edge`](../tests/top/test_top_recovery.py#L14) | active | Edits source during the final audit callback after a provider result exists. Requires transaction rollback of both the origin claim and its dependency edge, preventing publication against changed evidence. |
| [`RecoveryStoreTests.test_a_pair_in_error_keeps_the_step_reply_and_closes_its_activity_failed`](../tests/top/test_top_recovery.py#L26) | active | Documented SYL-289 regression: a provider error previously closed activity as refused without an error and lost the recovery reply. Checks both the emitted partial-progress reply and persisted failed activity. |
| [`RelationOccurrenceRegressionStoreTests.test_mixed_closed_and_unclosed_same_ref_pins_valid_occurrence_and_reports_only_invalid_fault`](../tests/top/test_top_relation_occurrence_regressions.py#L12) | active | Places valid and unclosed declarations of the same source in one document. Checks that occurrence-local validity creates one legitimate pin, reports only the malformed occurrence as faulty, and leaves reads non-mutating. |
| [`RelationSelectionRegressionStoreTests.test_same_literal_short_method_ref_cannot_redirect_existing_pin_to_unrelated_place`](../tests/top/test_top_relation_selection_regressions.py#L11) | active | Replaces a previously pinned class with an unrelated class exposing the same short method name. Requires publication refusal and unchanged persisted relation state instead of silently redirecting the pin. |
| [`SourceAddressTests.test_double_encoded_sequences_remain_literal_after_one_decode`](../tests/top/test_top_source_refs.py#L11) | active | Uses double-encoded separators, traversal spelling and a name delimiter. Requires exactly one decode and a stable canonical address, protecting literal names from accidental reinterpretation. |
| [`ProductStoreTests.test_exact_utf8_nul_bytes_projection_and_hash_guards_roundtrip`](../tests/top/test_top_store_acceptance.py#L14) | active | Regression for quadratic dense-NUL projection: 100,000 NUL bytes must project within a finite SQL deadline. Also checks byte-preserving storage and independent database constraints on hashes and projections. |
